# V2.05 Stitch Screen & Implementation Registry

Generated from:
- 12 Stitch projects
- consolidated Stitch inventory (`/tmp/stitch_inv/INVENTORY.txt`)
- V5 source code (routes, views, services) and prior V2.03/V2.04 Stitch audits

Branch: **V5**

Baseline: **V4 / Version 2.04**

Purpose: Single source of truth for Stitch designs and implementation status.

**Important:** A Stitch design marked COMPLETE means **DESIGN** complete. It does **NOT** mean application implementation complete.

## Inventory validation

See **Latest Stitch Metadata Sync** for the V2.05 35-screen metadata audit validation (project `12480486228004770375`). Historical 578-row inventory is retained.

- EXPECTED_INVENTORY_COUNT=578
- ACTUAL_INVENTORY_COUNT=578
- COUNT_MATCH=YES
- SUPPLEMENTAL_KNOWN_RECENT_DESIGNS=23 (listed separately; not counted in inventory total)
- Mapping method: PRODUCT + FEATURE PURPOSE + SCREEN TITLE + existing application route/view (not numeric ID alone).
- Implementation judgments are **conservative**: functional routes usually map to `IMPLEMENTED_PARTIAL` until visual/responsive parity is proven. No screen is marked `IMPLEMENTED_COMPLETE` in this baseline.

### Validation principles

- Stitch authoritative for: visual existence, title, viewport, layout, visible controls.
- Repository/requirements authoritative for: feature purpose, route, backend, implementation status, permissions, terminology, compliance, workflow semantics.
- `STITCH_STATUS=COMPLETE` does **not** imply `IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS=IMPLEMENTED_COMPLETE`.

### Admin Console canonical rules

- POST_REGISTRATION_LANDING=`Dashboard`
- POST_LOGIN_LANDING=`Dashboard`
- Canonical primary navigation (1–10): Dashboard, Website, Content, People, Operations, Locations, Media, Reports, Access, Settings
- Website Management is part of the Admin Console (not a detached administration application).
- `SHARED_PATTERN=AdminConsoleShell` for **AC-ADM-01** and **BB-ADM-01**
- `SHARED_PATTERN=WebsiteManagementHub` for **AC-WEB-ADM-01** and **BB-WEB-ADM-01**

### Theme screens canonical rules

- Applies to **AC-WEB-THM-01** and **BB-WEB-THM-01**
- Purpose: Select and preview website presentation themes without changing underlying content data.
- Themes: Clarity, Editorial, Community
- Workflow: Select Theme → Live Preview → Apply to Draft → Change Manager → Publish
- Responsive preview: Desktop 1440 / Tablet 768 / Mobile 390
- `SHARED_PATTERN=ThemeSelector+LivePreview`

## 7. Shared platform opportunities

Platform owns reusable infrastructure. Product owns design tokens, terminology, domain semantics, and clinical/church-specific behavior. **Do not merge AC and BB design tokens.**

| SHARED_COMPONENT | BB_SCREEN(S) | AC_SCREEN(S) | CURRENT_SHARED_STATUS | RECOMMENDATION |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| WebsiteManagementHub | BB-WM-*; WE01 hub | AC-MW-H01 | Partial shared website settings routes | Keep platform hub shell; product tiles/labels |
| MediaLibrary | BB-WM-M01/M02; Shared media picker | AC-MW-H04; AC-MW-E03 | Platform media engine in use | Continue shared media APIs; product chrome tokens |
| ImageEditor | Shared crop/position; WE01-03 | AC-MW-E03 | Shared WE01 GpUniversalImageEditor (Batch4) | Platform crop/position; product framing |
| VersionHistory | BB-WM-H01; Shared history/restore | AC-MW-H05 | Platform history/restore services | Shared history model; product copy |
| HistoricalPreview | Shared version details | AC history preview | Partial | Platform preview frame |
| RestoreDialog | Shared field history and restore | AC restore flows | Partial | Shared restore confirm primitive |
| ChangeManager | Shared unpublished changes / 5-change reminder | AC unpublished changes | Partial | Platform change budget UX |
| ResponsiveViewport | Shared D/M preview | AC/BB editor preview | Implemented Batch3 | Shared viewport toggle 1440/768/390 |
| InlineTextEditor | WE01 inline/structured text | AC/BB editable fields | Shared WE01 (Batch4) | Keep single engine; product content |
| MediaPicker | Shared media upload/library | AC/BB media field | website-media-field.js (Batch4) | Do not invent second media system |
| SectionManager | Add/edit/reorder/remove | AC/BB section actions | Shared section ops (Batch4) | Existing section model only |
| VideoEmbedEditor | Safe YouTube/Vimeo URL embed | BB hero/video; presentation video | platform/videoEmbedEditor.js (Batch4) | No raw iframe; no autoplay default |
| SaveBar | WE01 toolbars | AC/BB editor toolbars | editor-chrome SaveBar (Batch4) | Draft-first; never auto-publish |
| EmptyState | SH02; many list empties | AC list empties | Ad hoc | Shared empty-state primitive (tokens per product) |
| LoadingState | Shared/auth component states | AC/BB shells | Partial | Shared loading skeleton |
| ErrorState | SH15; auth states | AC access restricted | Partial | Shared error/access shells |

## 8. Notes on duplicates / canonical selection

- Duplicate groups keep all instances for traceability.
- `CANONICAL` preferred for COMPLETE + refined/populated titles; others marked `VARIANT` or `OBSOLETE` with `CANONICAL_SCREEN=<project>/<screen>` when applicable.
- Stitch `STATUS=VARIANT` rows are retained and flagged.
- Empty project `2734098283637220752` is retained with screen count 0.
- Known recent designs missing from the inventory snapshot are tracked in §2b and backlog only.
